(출처: 2026-07-21 Irene «구현·테스트 검증은 무조건 Fable» → 2026-09-06 «꼭 필요한 부분에만» → 2026-10-08 공용 기준으로 통일.) - **fableplan**: Fable plans in a fresh read-only subagent, the session executes. Delegates the design of a nontrivial change to the fable-planner subagent (Fable, read-only), then implements the approved plan here in the main session (Fable 5.1 by default; identical on Opus). Sol (Codex/GPT-5.6) critiques the plan, can be consulted mid-plan, and can be handed genuinely independent execution chunks. Use this whenever a task needs a plan before code gets written — the user asking to plan, plan mode, or any multi-file / architectural / "how should we do this" change — not only when invoked by name. ([ricalanis/orchestratormaxxing](https://github.com/ricalanis/orchestratormaxxing/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/fableplan.md))

- **fabrico-analyze-aws-costs**: Perform a comprehensive AWS cost optimization and tagging compliance audit — checks IaC code first, then validates everything against live infrastructure via API. ([ChrisCollinDev/fabrico-collections](https://github.com/ChrisCollinDev/fabrico-collections/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/fabrico-analyze-aws-costs.md) · ★2)
- **fabrico-analyze-gcp-costs**: Perform a comprehensive GCP cost optimization and labeling compliance audit — checks IaC code first, then validates everything against live infrastructure via API. ([ChrisCollinDev/fabrico-collections](https://github.com/ChrisCollinDev/fabrico-collections/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/fabrico-analyze-gcp-costs.md) · ★2)
- **fabrico-analyze-materials**: Process discovery workshop materials and create Jira-ready epics and user stories, or iterate on an existing Jira backlog. ([ChrisCollinDev/fabrico-collections](https://github.com/ChrisCollinDev/fabrico-collections/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/fabrico-analyze-materials.md) · ★2)
- **fabrico-audit-infrastructure**: Audit infrastructure for security gaps, cost waste, and best practices violations. ([ChrisCollinDev/fabrico-collections](https://github.com/ChrisCollinDev/fabrico-collections/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/fabrico-audit-infrastructure.md) · ★2)
- **fabrico-autopilot**: Autonomously build working software from a complete spec file: backlog → architecture → plan → implement every story → test → review, end to end with minimal interruptions. ([ChrisCollinDev/fabrico-collections](https://github.com/ChrisCollinDev/fabrico-collections/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/fabrico-autopilot.md) · ★2)
- **fabrico-create-custom-agent**: Create a new custom subagent (.claude/agents/*.md) for Claude Code. Analyzes existing subagents for patterns, guides through design decisions, creates the subagent file, and validates against best practices. ([ChrisCollinDev/fabrico-collections](https://github.com/ChrisCollinDev/fabrico-collections/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/fabrico-create-custom-agent.md) · ★2)
- **fabrico-create-custom-instructions**: Create Claude Code memory (CLAUDE.md) for a project. Analyzes existing project conventions, determines the appropriate memory scope and placement, creates the CLAUDE.md file(s), and validates against best practices. ([ChrisCollinDev/fabrico-collections](https://github.com/ChrisCollinDev/fabrico-collections/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/fabrico-create-custom-instructions.md) · ★2)
- **fabrico-create-custom-prompt**: Create a new custom slash command (.claude/commands/*.md) for Claude Code. Analyzes existing commands for patterns, identifies the right subagent to delegate to, creates the command file, and validates the workflow end-to-end. ([ChrisCollinDev/fabrico-collections](https://github.com/ChrisCollinDev/fabrico-collections/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/fabrico-create-custom-prompt.md) · ★2)
- **fabrico-create-custom-skill**: Create a new custom skill (.claude/skills/&lt;name&gt;/SKILL.md) for Claude Code. Analyzes existing skills for patterns, guides through design decisions, creates the skill file with supporting resources, and validates against best practices. ([ChrisCollinDev/fabrico-collections](https://github.com/ChrisCollinDev/fabrico-collections/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/fabrico-create-custom-skill.md) · ★2)
- **fabrico-create-spec**: Turn a one-line product idea into a complete, build-ready SPEC.md — the format /fabrico-autopilot builds from. Asks a few high-leverage clarifying questions, then fills the full spec template with concrete user stories and acceptance criteria. ([ChrisCollinDev/fabrico-collections](https://github.com/ChrisCollinDev/fabrico-collections/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/fabrico-create-spec.md) · ★2)
- **fabrico-deploy-kubernetes**: Creates Kubernetes deployments, Helm charts, and workload configurations following production-ready patterns for high availability, resource management, and secrets handling. Ensures consistent deployment practices across environments with proper health probes, scaling policies, and security configurations. ([ChrisCollinDev/fabrico-collections](https://github.com/ChrisCollinDev/fabrico-collections/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/fabrico-deploy-kubernetes.md) · ★2)
- **fabrico-engineer-prompt**: Design, optimize, audit, or review LLM application prompts (system prompts, user prompt templates, RAG templates, agent instructions, classification prompts) for quality, security, and consistency.
